A few things I deliberately included that make it stand out to recruiters:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>The "Known limitations" section</strong> is the one most people would leave out — but it's the one that impresses experienced engineers most. Being upfront that gaze tracking isn't perfect, and explaining <em>why</em> AI flags are advisory rather than automatic proof, shows engineering maturity and ethical awareness. Most portfolio projects overclaim; yours doesn't.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>The security hardening section</strong> documents everything you did beyond "making it work" — that's the difference between a student project and a production-minded one.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>Setup instructions cover the real gotchas</strong> you hit: the model files that must be downloaded separately, and the warning that Docker and local can't run on the same ports simultaneously.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">Refresh your GitHub page and the README will render below the file list.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>One thing worth adding later:</strong> screenshots. A recruiter scanning your repo will linger on images far longer than text. If you take 3-4 screenshots (the dark login page, the exam page with the webcam and alerts, the admin results table, the violation report), create a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">screenshots/</code> folder and embed them in the README with <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">![Login](screenshots/login.png)</code>. That would make it genuinely eye-catching.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">Want help adding a screenshots section, or is this good to push as-is?</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-2 py-2"><div opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-[var(--msg-assistant-pb,0.75rem)]" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 data-find-omitted="" class="sr-only select-none">Claude responded: Git can't find the file — you need to actually save it into your project folder first.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">Git can't find the file — you need to actually save it into your project folder first. Downloading it from this chat doesn't put it there.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">Two ways to do this:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>Option A — download and move it:</strong></p>

<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Click the README file I shared above to download it</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Move it from your Downloads folder into <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">C:\Users\LENOVO\proctoring-system\</code></li>
</ol>

<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">In VS Code, right-click in the file explorer at the <strong>project root</strong> (<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">PROCTORING-SYSTEM</code>, not inside backend or frontend)</li>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">Since you're changing the name, a few other places will still say "PlaceMux" that you'll want to update for consistency — especially if you're showing this in interviews:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>In your app's frontend</strong>, the navbar brand appears in these files:</p>

<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ManageStudents.jsx</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Login.jsx</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Signup.jsx</code></li>
</ul>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal">In each, search (<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Ctrl + F</code>) for <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">PlaceMux</code> and replace with <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ProctorAI</code>. VS Code can do this across all files at once: press <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Ctrl + Shift + F</code> (search across files), type <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">PlaceMux</code>, then click the arrow to expand the replace box, type <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ProctorAI</code>, and click "Replace All."</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal"><strong>Also consider renaming the GitHub repo itself</strong> if you want — currently it's <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">AI-Powered-Assessment-Proctoring-Platform</code>, which is actually a good descriptive name and doesn't need changing. But if you'd rather it be <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">proctorai</code>, you can rename it in GitHub → Settings → Repository name (Git will handle the redirect).</p>

gap-3 font-claude-response"><h1 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.375rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="1:1-1:12;0-11">ProctorAI</h1>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="3:1-3:48;13-60"><strong>AI-Powered Assessment &amp; Proctoring Platform</strong></p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="5:1-5:250;62-311">A full-stack technical assessment platform that lets organisations run secure online exams while automatically detecting cheating through two independent layers — browser lockdown and real-time AI monitoring of the candidate's webcam and microphone.</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="7:1-7:105;313-417">Built with React, FastAPI, and PostgreSQL. Fully containerized, tested, and hardened for production use.</p>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="11:1-11:16;424-439">What it does</h2>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="13:1-13:295;441-735">Companies screening technical candidates need to run exams online, but can't have a human watching every candidate's webcam. ProctorAI solves this: it runs the exam, scores it securely on the server, and simultaneously monitors the candidate for cheating — flagging violations for human review.</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="15:1-15:226;737-962">If a candidate switches tabs, exits fullscreen, looks away from the screen, has another person appear behind them, pulls out a phone, or starts talking, the system detects it, warns them, and records it in an auditable trail.</p>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="19:1-19:12;969-980">Features</h2>

<h3 class="text-text-100 mt-2 -mb-1 text-base font-bold" data-sourcepos="21:1-21:22;982-1003">Assessment engine</h3>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="22:1-27:34;1004-1446">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="22:1-22:100;1004-1103">Question bank with randomised selection — each candidate receives a different subset of questions</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="23:1-23:97;1104-1200">Answer options shuffled per question, with text-based scoring so shuffling can't break grading</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="24:1-24:65;1201-1265">Server-side scoring (the client is never trusted with grading)</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="25:1-25:85;1266-1350">Server-locked exam sessions: refreshing won't re-roll questions or pause the timer</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="26:1-26:62;1351-1412">Countdown timer with automatic submission when time expires</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="27:1-27:34;1413-1446">Duplicate-submission protection</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="text-text-100 mt-2 -mb-1 text-base font-bold" data-sourcepos="29:1-29:43;1448-1490">Proctoring — Layer 1: Browser security</h3>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="30:1-35:82;1491-1870">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="30:1-30:46;1491-1536">Tab-switch detection via the Visibility API</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="31:1-31:45;1537-1581">Fullscreen enforcement with exit detection</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="32:1-32:45;1582-1626">Copy, paste, cut, and right-click blocking</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="33:1-33:60;1627-1686">Keyboard shortcut blocking (Alt, Meta, F11/F12, DevTools)</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="34:1-34:102;1687-1788">Graded violation levels: Level 1 (minor, dismissible warning) and Level 2 (serious, blocking modal)</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="35:1-35:82;1789-1870">Three Level 2 violations automatically terminate the exam, enforced server-side</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="text-text-100 mt-2 -mb-1 text-base font-bold" data-sourcepos="37:1-37:40;1872-1911">Proctoring — Layer 2: AI monitoring</h3>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="38:1-42:99;1912-2305">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="38:1-38:77;1912-1988"><strong>Face detection</strong> — verifies a candidate is present; flags multiple faces</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="39:1-39:76;1989-2064"><strong>Gaze tracking</strong> — detects when the candidate looks away from the screen</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="40:1-40:55;2065-2119"><strong>Lip movement detection</strong> — flags possible speaking</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="41:1-41:87;2120-2206"><strong>Object detection</strong> — identifies phones, books, laptops, and other prohibited items</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="42:1-42:99;2207-2305"><strong>Audio monitoring</strong> — voice-frequency analysis to detect talking, tuned to ignore ambient noise</li>
</ul>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="44:1-44:128;2307-2434">Frames are captured every 1.5 seconds, sent to the backend, analysed, and any violation is logged and surfaced as a live alert.</p>

<h3 class="text-text-100 mt-2 -mb-1 text-base font-bold" data-sourcepos="46:1-46:18;2436-2453">Admin console</h3>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="47:1-51:79;2454-2808">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="47:1-47:60;2454-2513">Create exams manually or by uploading a CSV question bank</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="48:1-48:73;2514-2586">Ranked candidate results with scores, percentages, and integrity flags</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="49:1-49:73;2587-2659">Detailed violation reports grouped per candidate, with severity badges</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="50:1-50:70;2660-2729">Student management: reset attempts, delete results, remove accounts</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="51:1-51:79;2730-2808">Structured audit log of logins, submissions, terminations, and admin actions</li>
</ul>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="55:1-55:14;2815-2828">Tech stack</h2>

<div class="overflow-x-auto w-full px-2 mb-6" data-sourcepos="57:1-66:21;2830-3247"><table class="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm leading-[1.7] whitespace-normal"><thead class="text-left"><tr><th border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.3)] py-2 pr-4 align-top">Backend</td><td class="border-b-0.5 border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.3)] py-2 pr-4 align-top">FastAPI (Python 3.12), Uvicorn</td></tr><tr><td align-top">Infrastructure</td><td class="border-b-0.5 border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.3)] py-2 pr-4 align-top">Docker, Docker Compose</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b-0.5 border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.3)] py-2 pr-4 align-top">Testing</td><td class="border-b-0.5 border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.3)] py-2 pr-4 align-top">pytest</td></tr></tbody></table></div>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="70:1-70:35;3254-3288">Security &amp; production hardening</h2>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="72:1-72:64;3290-3353">This project was deliberately taken beyond "working prototype":</p>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="74:1-81:59;3355-3997">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="74:1-74:101;3355-3455">Secrets (database credentials, JWT signing key) loaded from environment variables, never committed</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="75:1-75:77;3456-3532">Admin accounts cannot be self-registered — created only by existing admins</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="76:1-76:85;3533-3617">Exam sessions locked server-side, preventing question re-rolling and timer pausing</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="77:1-77:70;3618-3687">Submissions validated against the candidate's assigned question set</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="78:1-78:51;3688-3738">Login rate-limited to block brute-force attempts</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="79:1-79:116;3739-3854">Authentication errors deliberately generic ("Invalid email or password") to avoid revealing which field was wrong</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="80:1-80:84;3855-3938">AI analysis is failure-isolated: a bad frame can never crash or interrupt an exam</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="81:1-81:59;3939-3997">Structured audit logging of all security-relevant events</li>
</ul>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="85:1-85:19;4004-4022">Getting started</h2>

<h3 class="text-text-100 mt-2 -mb-1 text-base font-bold" data-sourcepos="87:1-87:18;4024-4041">Prerequisites</h3>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="88:1-89:71;4042-4167">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="88:1-88:55;4042-4096">Docker Desktop (for the containerised setup), <strong>or</strong></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="89:1-89:71;4097-4167">Python 3.12+, Node.js 20+, and PostgreSQL 16 (for local development)</li>
</ul>

<blockquote class="ml-2 border-l-4 border-[hsl(var(--border-300)/0.1)] pl-4 text-text-300" data-sourcepos="154:1-154:108;5598-5705">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="154:3-154:108;5600-5705"><strong>Note:</strong> Docker and the local setup use the same ports. Run one or the other, never both simultaneously.</p>

</blockquote>

</span></span><span>pytest -v</span></code></pre></div></div>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="165:1-165:232;5768-5999">The suite covers password hashing and salting, JWT generation, signup and login flows, access control on protected routes, and exam scoring — including a test verifying that scoring remains correct when answer options are shuffled.</p>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="169:1-169:21;6006-6026">Known limitations</h2>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="171:1-171:218;6028-6245">Deployed AI that affects people's opportunities deserves honesty about its accuracy. These limitations are inherent to the problem, not implementation defects — commercial proctoring systems face the same constraints:</p>

<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3" data-sourcepos="173:1-175:107;6247-6742">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="173:1-173:193;6247-6439"><strong>Gaze tracking</strong> works well but is not perfectly accurate. True eye-tracking varies with camera angle, lighting, and individual eye shape; high precision would require per-user calibration.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="174:1-174:196;6440-6635"><strong>Audio detection</strong> distinguishes speech from ambient noise using voice-band frequency analysis, but a whisper and background noise occupy overlapping ranges. Thresholds are a tuned compromise.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2" data-sourcepos="175:1-175:107;6636-6742"><strong>Lip movement detection</strong> flags an open or moving mouth, but cannot confirm speech from a single frame.</li>
</ul>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="177:1-177:288;6744-7031">For this reason, AI-detected flags are presented to administrators as <strong>advisory signals for human review</strong>, not as automatic proof of misconduct. The admin interface states this explicitly. Only browser-security violations — which are deterministic — trigger automatic exam termination.</p>

<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<h2 class="text-text-100 mt-3 -mb-1 text-[1.125rem] font-bold" data-sourcepos="181:1-181:20;7038-7057">Deployment notes</h2>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" data-sourcepos="183:1-183:219;7059-7277">The backend loads PyTorch, MediaPipe, and YOLOv8, requiring roughly <strong>2 GB of RAM</strong>. Free hosting tiers typically provide 256–512 MB, which is insufficient. Production deployment requires a server with adequate memory.</p>
